### [1] [GAP3D: Generative Alignment of VLM Latents to Patch-Level Embeddings for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28995)
*Polytimi Anna Gkotsi,Andrii Zadaianchuk,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GAP3D提出了一种模块化的扩散对齐方法，将VLM生成的latents直接对齐到预训练图像编码器的patch级特征空间，使得冻结的下游生成模型（如3D资产生成）能利用VLM作为提示编码器，同时保留稠密的空间结构信息。该方法主要通过通用域图像-文本对训练，无需大规模3D数据，并展现出对多模态提示的零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将VLM作为生成模型提示编码器的方法要么依赖昂贵的端到端训练，要么将特征映射到压缩表示，丢弃了稠密空间结构，这对于需要几何感知的任务（如3D资产生成）至关重要。

Method: 提出GAP3D，一种基于扩散的模块化对齐方法，将VLM生成的latents直接对齐到预训练图像编码器完整的patch级特征空间，使冻结的下游生成模型能够利用VLM作为提示编码器，同时保持空间结构化的条件信号。

Result: 在3D资产生成任务上，仅通过通用域图像-文本对训练即可绕过大规模3D数据的依赖；展现出对多模态提示的零样本能力（尽管仅用文本输入训练）；但当前方法在精细化细节方面仍有不足，更侧重于高层语义。

Conclusion: VLM与图像编码器特征空间之间的表示差距可以通过扩散对齐部分弥合，为通过生成式对齐到稠密嵌入空间的基础模型模块化集成迈出了第一步。

Abstract: Recent approaches integrating vision-language models (VLMs) as prompt encoders for generative model conditioning typically rely on expensive end-to-end training or map features to compressed representations, discarding the dense spatial structure required for geometry-aware tasks like 3D asset generation. To address this, we propose GAP3D, a modular, diffusion-based approach that aligns VLM-generated latents directly to the complete, patch-level feature space of a pre-trained image encoder, enabling a frozen downstream generative model to utilize a VLM as prompt encoder while maintaining a spatially structured conditioning signal. Evaluated on 3D asset generation, our method bypasses the need for large-scale 3D data by training mainly on general-domain image-text pairs. It also exhibits emergent zero-shot capabilities for multimodal prompts, despite being trained exclusively on text input. Finally, while currently prioritizing high-level semantics over fine-grained detail, GAP3D demonstrates that the representation gap between VLM and image-encoder feature spaces can be partially bridged through diffusion-based alignment, taking the first steps towards a modular integration of foundation models through generative alignment to dense embedding spaces.

</details>


### [2] [Auditing Training-Free 3D Shape Retrieval with Diffused Geodesic Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29004)
*Zhicheng Du,Changyue Liu,Wenji Xi,Zhaotian Xie,Zhuo Deng,Ziheng Zhang,Yang Liu,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散测地矩（DGM）描述符，并通过对训练无关的形状描述符进行协议审计，揭示了输入场和聚合协议对评估结果的支配性影响，表明现有评分常混淆局部信号设计、归一化、聚合、码本拟合和度量选择等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的形状描述符的检索评分混淆了局部信号设计、归一化、聚合、码本拟合和度量选择等多种因素，导致难以对单一组件进行独立评估。为此，本文重新将描述符评估框架定义为"协议审计"，旨在解耦这些因素的影响。

Method: 提出扩散测地矩（DGM）描述符，一种基于种子的条件描述符，通过计算稀疏隐式热响应，将其转换为距离场，并跨种子和尺度聚合每个顶点的低阶矩。DGM既作为非谱基线，也作为隔离协议效应的工具。在FAUST-Reg和TOSCA数据集上进行聚合匹配实验，并与基于热核信号（HKS）的几何矩形状描述符（GMSD-HKS）及波核信号（WKS）进行对比。

Result: 在FAUST-Reg和TOSCA数据集上，GMSD-HKS取得最高分数（mAP/top-1: 0.621/0.820和0.865/0.963），WKS仍为强经典信号，DGM主要在稀疏求解、非谱部署或对称信息种子帧为首要需求时有用。方法论发现：输入场和聚合协议可主导矩公式的影响。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了可复现的协议级联分析、跨形状对齐诊断工具（用于功能映射兼容性），以及针对训练无关形状描述符设计和报告的具体建议。核心结论是：描述符评估中，输入场和聚合协议的选择比矩公式本身更具支配性影响。

Abstract: Reported retrieval scores for training-free shape descriptors conflate local signal design, normalization, aggregation, codebook fitting, and metric choices, making isolated component evaluation difficult. This paper reframes descriptor evaluation as a {\em protocol audit}. We introduce Diffused Geodesic Moments (DGM), a seed-conditioned descriptor that computes sparse implicit heat responses, converts them to distance-like fields, and summarizes each vertex by low-order moments across seeds and scales. DGM is used both as a practical non-spectral baseline and as an instrument for isolating protocol effects. On the registered FAUST benchmark split (FAUST-Reg) and the TOSCA shape collection, aggregation-matched experiments show that an independent Geometric Moment Shape Descriptor baseline built on Heat Kernel Signature features (GMSD-HKS) obtains the highest scores in this implementation ($0.621/0.820$ and $0.865/0.963$ mean average precision (mAP)/top-1), Wave Kernel Signature (WKS) remains a strong classical signal, and DGM is useful mainly when sparse solves, non-spectral deployment, or symmetry-informative seed frames are priorities. The broader finding is methodological: the input field and aggregation protocol can dominate the moment formula. The paper contributes a reproducible protocol-cascade analysis, a cross-shape alignment diagnostic for functional-map compatibility, and concrete recommendations for designing and reporting training-free shape descriptors.

</details>


### [3] [Embodied3DBench: Benchmarking Low-Level Embodied Spatial Intelligence of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29074)
*Jiyao Zhang,Mingxu Zhang,Yitong Peng,Haoxuan Liu,Chenshuo Wang,Yuxing Long,Haoyang Huang,Dongjiang Li,Nan Duan,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Embodied3DBench基准，包含6个任务类别（空间结构理解与交互导向感知）、12个子类别和2.1万+问答对，评估13个VLM模型后发现模型在高层空间推理较强，但在交互导向感知上脆弱。为解决该问题，合成了130万QA对的训练数据，微调后显著提升了低层空间智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D具身环境中的低层空间智能（特别是交互导向感知）能力尚未被系统评估，缺乏针对性的基准和训练数据。

Method: 构建了包含6个任务类别（分为空间结构理解与交互导向感知两个核心组）的Embodied3DBench基准，评估13个SOTA模型；并合成了130万QA对的训练数据集用于微调。

Result: 当前VLM在高层空间推理（如物体间位置关系）表现较好，但在交互导向感知任务上表现脆弱；通过130万QA数据集微调，低层空间智能获得显著提升。

Conclusion: Embodied3DBench填补了具身环境下VLM低层空间智能系统评估的空白，同时提供了可扩展的数据解决方案，为构建交互感知多模态系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Are current Vision Language Models (VLMs) ready to comprehend and reason about complex embodied interactions in 3D environments? We introduce Embodied3DBench, a robot-centric benchmark targeting low-level spatial intelligence in embodied 3D environments. To systematically evaluate these foundational perceptual capabilities, the benchmark includes 6 task categories divided into two core groups: Spatial Structural Understanding (Grounding, Spatial Relation Prediction, and Multi-view Correspondence) and Interaction-Oriented Perception (Affordance Prediction, Grasp Point Prediction, and Trajectory Prediction). The benchmark spans 12 subcategories and contains over 21k high-quality question-answer pairs. We evaluate 13 state-of-the-art models, and the results show that while current models exhibit relatively strong high-level spatial reasoning, such as understanding object-to-object positional relations, they remain fragile in interaction-oriented perception, highlighting a significant lack of robust 3D-aware interaction priors. To actively bridge this capability gap revealed by our benchmark, we further synthesize a large-scale training dataset comprising 1.3M QA pairs. Notably, fine-tuning on this dataset yields significant improvements in low-level spatial intelligence. Ultimately, Embodied3DBench fills a critical gap by providing both a systematic evaluation framework and a scalable data solution, setting a clear target for the development of interaction-aware multimodal systems.

</details>


### [4] [GeRaF: Neural Geometry Reconstruction from Radio Frequency Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29097)
*Jiachen Lu,Hailan Shanbhag,Haitham Al Hassanieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeRaF首次利用神经隐式学习从射频信号中实现近场三维几何重建，通过基于滤波的渲染、物理射频体渲染管线以及无透镜采样与alpha混合策略，克服了射频信号低分辨率、高噪声和镜面反射等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB和LiDAR方法受限于遮挡问题，而RF信号虽能穿透遮挡但存在无透镜成像固有的低分辨率、高噪声问题，且RF信号通过镜面反射与表面交互，需要全新的建模方式。

Method: (1) 基于滤波的渲染以抑制无关信号；(2) 物理感知的RF体渲染管线；(3) 无透镜采样与无透镜alpha混合策略，实现训练期间的全空间高效采样；通过MLP学习带符号距离函数、反射率和信号功率。

Result: 首次实现从真实世界RF信号中重建毫米级几何结构。

Conclusion: GeRaF为基于RF信号的近场三维重建开辟了新方向，证明了神经隐式学习在无透镜、高噪声、镜面反射场景下的可行性。

Abstract: GeRaF is the first method to use neural implicit learning for near-range 3D geometry reconstruction from radio frequency (RF) signals. Unlike RGB or LiDAR-based methods, RF sensing can see through occlusion but suffers from low resolution and noise due to its lensless imaging nature. While lenses in RGB imaging constrain sampling to 1D rays, RF signals propagate through the entire space, introducing significant noise and leading to cubic complexity in volumetric rendering. Moreover, RF signals interact with surfaces via specular reflections, requiring fundamentally different modeling. To address these challenges, GeRaF (1) introduces filter-based rendering to suppress irrelevant signals, (2) implements a physics-based RF volumetric rendering pipeline, and (3) proposes a novel lensless sampling and lensless alpha blending strategy that makes full-space sampling feasible during training. By learning signed distance functions, reflectiveness, and signal power through MLPs and trainable parameters, GeRaF takes the first step towards reconstructing millimeter-level geometry from RF signals in real-world settings.

</details>


### [5] [Seeing through boxes: Non-Line-of-Sight 3D Reconstruction from Radar Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29098)
*Jiachen Lu,Hailan Shanbhag,Haitham Al Hassanieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeRaF 2.0 提出了统一视距（LoS）和非视距（NLoS）的神经几何重建框架，利用外部 LoS 几何约束来引导射频信号传播建模，实现了对可见和隐藏场景的物理一致重建，在射频几何重建领域达到了新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 NLoS 3D 神经重建方法受限于不稳定优化、噪声表面几何和表面歧义问题，无法从 SDF 生成精确的零水平集，根源在于忽略了封闭区域外的 LoS 几何所提供的物理约束。

Method: 提出了 GeRaF 2.0 框架，将外部 LoS 几何先验集成到神经场公式中，利用 LoS 区域几何约束来建模和引导射频信号从 LoS 区域向 NLoS 区域的传播过程。

Result: 实现了稳定训练和物理一致的重建，在可见和隐藏几何的重建上均达到射频几何重建领域的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过统一 LoS 和 NLoS 几何约束，有效解决了现有方法中优化不稳定、表面噪声和歧义问题，验证了引入物理一致的传播建模对于射频几何重建的重要性。

Abstract: Reconstructing object geometry from radio frequency (RF) signals is fundamentally challenging due to the lensless imaging nature of RF sensing, which leads to low spatial resolution and high noise. Unlike light signals, RF signals can penetrate occlusions and thus capture information about hidden scenes. Existing Non-Line-of-Sight (NLoS) 3D neural reconstruction methods can recover coarse surfaces inside enclosed environments but often suffer from unstable optimization, noisy surface geometry, and surface ambiguity, failing to produce accurate zero-level sets from the signed distance field (SDF). These limitations largely stem from neglecting the role of Line-of-Sight (LoS) geometry outside the enclosed region, which provides valuable physical constraints for modeling signal propagation. In this paper, we introduce a Unified LoS and NLoS neural geometry reconstruction framework GeRaF 2.0 that leverages the outside LoS geometry to model and guide RF propagation from the LoS region into the NLoS region. By integrating visual LoS priors into the neural field formulation, GeRaF 2.0 achieves stable training and physically consistent reconstruction of both visible and hidden geometry, setting a new state-of-the-art in RF-based geometry reconstruction.

</details>


### [6] [Robust Cross-Domain Generalization Using Unlabeled Target Data with Source-Domain Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29122)
*Yuyue Zhou,Shrimanti Ghosh,Michael,Xie,Justin JY Kim,Jessica Knight,Steel McDonald,Vincent Man,Jacob L. Jaremko,Abhilash Hareendranathan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种目标域自监督预训练与模型集成策略，在无需目标域标注的情况下，显著提升了儿科腕部超声骨折分割模型在不同设备间的泛化性能（Dice提升超6%），实现了标签高效且隐私保护的跨设备超声AI框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像AI模型在不同超声设备或临床站点间存在严重的域偏移问题，而重新标注新设备数据因人工成本高和隐私限制而难以实现，亟需一种无需目标域标签即可提升跨设备泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出目标域感知的自监督预训练与模型集成策略：结合掩码图像建模（MIM）和对比学习在无标签目标域数据上学习结构表征，引入置信度感知的融合头（confidence-aware infusion head）自适应整合预测结果；源域数据（Philips Lumify探头）用于有监督训练，目标域数据（TeleMED探头）严格保持无标签状态。

Result: 在来自62个儿科POCUS视频的318张图像上，该方法在目标域上相比基线实现了超过6%的Dice系数提升，显著改善了跨设备性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种标签高效且隐私保护的跨设备鲁棒超声AI框架，可扩展至多中心研究或联邦学习场景，为解决医学影像模型域泛化问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: It is often desirable to generalize medical imaging AI models trained with dense annotations to data acquired from different ultrasound scanners or clinical sites; however, retraining these models with new annotations is often difficult and costly. We examine this challenge in pediatric wrist fracture assessment using point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS), where fractures are common and can be effectively triaged via ultrasound. AI has shown radiologist-level performance for fracture detection, often aided by high-quality bony structure segmentation. However, due to significant domain shifts, models perform poorly on data from other centers or probes, and obtaining segmentation labels across devices is impractical due to manual annotation effort and data privacy concerns. To address this, we propose a target-informed self-supervised pretraining and model-ensemble strategy. Specifically, our approach combines masked image modeling (MIM) and contrastive learning to learn target-domain structural representations without labels, and introduces a confidence-aware infusion head to adaptively integrate predictions. The source dataset, collected with a Philips Lumify probe, contained dense labels, while the target dataset, acquired with a TeleMED portable probe, was unlabeled. The datasets were kept strictly separate throughout the entire process. Our method used labeled source data for supervised training and leveraged target-domain pretraining to improve generalization. On 318 images from 62 pediatric POCUS videos, this approach significantly improved cross-device performance, achieving over 6% Dice improvement on the target domain versus the baseline. These results demonstrate a label-efficient and privacy-preserving approach for cross-device-robust ultrasound AI, offering a framework that can be extended to multi-center studies or federated learning setups.

</details>


### [7] [Eulerian Gaussian Splatting using Hashed Probability Pyramids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29136)
*Mia Gaia Polansky,George Kopanas,Stephan Garbin,Todd Zickler,Dor Verbin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于概率抛雪球（splat）的辐射场框架，用可学习概率密度的梯度优化替代3DGS中手工设计的图元操作（如移动、分裂、剔除），通过多尺度层级网格实例化密度并推导无偏梯度估计器，在保持3DGS渲染速度的同时在mip-NeRF 360上达到SOTA重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS依赖手工设计的稠密化策略（如ADC）来管理高斯原语，这些启发式方法缺乏理论保证且需要精细调参，限制了模型的泛化能力和优化稳定性。作者希望用基于概率密度的端到端梯度优化替代这些人工规则，实现更自然、更鲁棒的场景表示学习。

Method: 1) 将高斯原语的位置视为从持久可学习概率密度中采样的样本；2) 提出一种内存高效的多尺度层级网格来实例化该密度；3) 推导基于控制变量的无偏梯度估计器以降低方差，稳定优化过程；4) 保留3DGS的快速光栅化管线以实现实时渲染。

Result: 在mip-NeRF 360数据集上达到SOTA的新视角合成重建质量，同时保持与3DGS相当的渲染速度。

Conclusion: 通过将启发式的图元管理转化为概率密度的梯度优化，成功消除了脆弱的先验假设，使概率质量能够自然地流向损失函数所需的位置，实现了重建质量与渲染速度的更好权衡。

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic splat-based radiance field framework that retains the fast rasterization and test-time efficiency of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) while replacing heuristic primitive manipulation with gradient-based optimization of a volumetric probability density. Rather than relocating, splitting, or culling Gaussians via hand-tuned densification (e.g., ADC), we treat primitive locations as samples drawn from a persistent, learnable density. We instantiate this density using a novel, memory-efficient multi-scale hierarchical grid that enables end-to-end gradient-based optimization. To stabilize the optimization, we derive an unbiased gradient estimator with control variates that markedly reduces variance. By allowing probability mass to flow to where the loss demands, our framework eliminates brittle priors and naturally explores the volume, achieving state-of-the-art reconstruction quality on mip-NeRF 360 while preserving 3DGS-level rendering speed.

</details>


### [8] [Guidance Contrastive Token Credit Assignment for Discrete Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29198)
*Shufan Li,Konstantinos Kallidromitis,Akash Gokul Yusuke Kato,Kazuki Kozuka,Aditya Grover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GCPO算法，通过对比正负提示下的模型预测实现逐令牌信用分配，克服了GRPO/DAPO等群体优势强化学习方法中均匀信用分配的局限性，在文生图和思维链推理任务上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体优势强化学习方法（如GRPO、DAPO）依赖样本级奖励，对所有令牌进行均匀信用分配，无法捕捉细粒度的令牌级贡献，限制了学习信号的精确性。

Method: 提出Guidance Contrastive Policy Optimization (GCPO)算法，通过对比正负提示下的模型预测差异，为每个令牌分配与其对比预测差异成比例的令牌级优势，实现更精确的信用分配。

Result: 在文生图任务中，GCPO能强调与文本提示对齐的视觉区域；在思维链推理任务中，能突出关键推理关键词。在文生图和思维链推理基准上，GCPO均一致优于GRPO和DAPO基线。

Conclusion: GCPO是一种通用且可扩展的离散策略学习优化策略，通过令牌级信用分配有效提升了强化学习在多种任务上的性能。

Abstract: Group-advantage-based reinforcement learning methods, such as GRPO and DAPO, have demonstrated strong performance across diverse domains, including mathematical reasoning and text-to-image generation. However, their reliance on sample-level rewards introduces a key limitation as uniform credit assignment across all tokens fails to capture fine-grained, token-level contributions. To address this issue, we propose Guidance Contrastive Policy Optimization (GCPO), a novel algorithm that enables per-token credit assignment by contrasting model predictions under positive and negative prompts. Rather than uniformly broadcasting sample-level advantages, GCPO assigns token-level advantages proportional to the difference between these contrastive predictions, allowing more precise and informative learning signals. Empirically, we find that GCPO emphasizes semantically relevant regions such as visual areas aligned with textual prompts in text-to-image generation, and critical keywords within reasoning traces for chain-of-thought tasks. Through extensive experiments, GCPO consistently outperforms GRPO and DAPO baselines on both text-to-image generation and chain-of-thought reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness as a general and scalable optimization strategy for discrete policy learning.

</details>


### [9] [MetaRanker: Human-in-the-loop Active Ranking for Metalens Image Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29212)
*Yujin Park,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaRanker，一个基于人机协同主动排序的框架，用于在超薄金属透镜成像系统中以语义可解释性（人类识别物体的能力）来评估图像质量，无需80%的成对标注，比传统PSNR等指标更符合人类感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金属透镜成像系统评估多采用PSNR等失真导向的客观指标，但这些指标与人类偏好和下游任务效用相关性弱，存在感知-失真权衡问题，亟需一种更符合人类感知的评估方法。

Method: MetaRanker框架结合概率偏好模型与不确定性感知的查询选择策略，利用视觉-语言模型提供轻量级语义先验来指导信息性比较的采样，同时以人类判断作为主要监督信号，采用主动学习减少标注量。

Result: 在真实和合成金属透镜数据集上，MetaRanker生成的排序与人类评估最一致，同时将所需成对标注数量减少约80%。标准图像质量评估指标在金属透镜领域与人类可解释性的一致性有限。

Conclusion: MetaRanker为金属透镜图像评估提供了一种感知驱动的实用方案，有望推动面向人类感知的金属透镜评估与协同设计。

Abstract: Image quality in modern imaging systems emerges from the coupled effects of the sensor, optics, and computational reconstruction. Ultra-thin metalenses offer a path toward substantial miniaturization of optical modules, but practical designs often exhibit pronounced chromatic and field-dependent aberrations that necessitate computational reconstruction. In current metalens pipelines, reconstruction models are commonly trained and selected using distortion-based fidelity objectives, such as PSNR, yet these proxies can be weakly correlated with human preference and downstream utility, reflecting the well-known perception--distortion trade-off. We introduce MetaRanker, a human-in-the-loop active ranking framework that formalizes metalens image quality in terms of semantic interpretability, defined as the degree to which humans can reliably recognize objects and structures in the presence of optical artifacts. MetaRanker combines a probabilistic preference model with uncertainty-aware query selection, and leverages vision--language models to provide lightweight semantic priors. Importantly, these priors are used only to guide the sampling of informative comparisons; human judgments remain the primary supervision signal throughout. Across real-world and synthetic metalens datasets with distinct degradation profiles, MetaRanker produces rankings that align most closely with human assessments, while reducing the number of pairwise annotations required by approximately 80% relative to exhaustive pairwise evaluation. Finally, we show that standard image quality assessment metrics exhibit limited alignment with human interpretability in the metalens domain, positioning MetaRanker as a practical step toward perceptually grounded metalens evaluation and co-design.

</details>


### [10] [Towards the automated segmentation of epicardial and mediastinal fats: A multi-manufacturer approach using intersubject registration and random forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29217)
*É. O. Rodrigues,A. Conci,F. F. C. Morais,M. G. Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据挖掘分类算法的全自动心脏脂肪组织分割方法，在CT图像上区分心外膜和纵隔脂肪，达到98.4%的平均准确率和96.8%的Dice相似系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏周围脂肪量与多种健康风险因素相关，且与全身脂肪无关，因此定量分析这些脂肪组织至关重要。现有方法需要大量人工干预，缺乏可重复性，亟需一种全自动、高精度的分割方法。

Method: 方法包括三个步骤：1) 图像配准，将输入图像粗调至标准空间；2) 提取像素及其邻域特征；3) 基于数据挖掘分类算法进行分割，判断每个像素的类别（心外膜脂肪、纵隔脂肪或背景）。

Result: 心外膜和纵隔脂肪的平均分割准确率为98.4%，平均真阳性率为96.2%，平均Dice相似指数为96.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够全自动、高精度地分割两种心脏脂肪组织，最小化用户干预，具有良好的可重复性，可辅助临床决策支持系统。

Abstract: The amount of fat on the surroundings of the heart is correlated to several health risk factors such as carotid stiffness, coronary artery calcification, atrial fibrillation, atherosclerosis, cancer incidence and others. Furthermore, the cardiac fat varies unrelated to the overall fat of the subject, and, therefore, it reinforces the quantitative analysis of these adipose tissues as being essential. Clinical decision support systems are computer programs capable of evaluating information and providing a corresponding diagnosis or data to complement the physicists' analyses. The aim of this work is to propose a method capable of fully automatically segmenting two types of cardiac adipose tissues that stand apart from each other by the pericardium on CT images obtained by the standard acquisition protocol used for coronary calcium scoring. Much effort was devoted to promote minimal user intervention and ease of reproducibility. The methodology proposed in this work consists of a registration, which will roughly adjust input images to a standard, an extraction of features related to pixels and their surrounding area and a segmentation step based on data mining classification algorithms that define if an incoming pixel is of a certain type. Experimentations showed that the achieved mean accuracy for the epicardial and mediastinal fats was 98.4% with a mean true positive rate of 96.2%. In average, the Dice similarity index was equal to 96.8%.

</details>


### [11] [Motion-guided sparse correction enables expert-quality point tracking across diverse microscopy regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29220)
*Leonidas Zimianitis,Pasindu Thenahandi,Kai Buckhalter,Dineth Jayakody,Julian O. Kimura,Xinyue Liang,Karen Cunningham,Azeem Ahmad,Balpreet S. Ahluwalia,Sampath Jayarathna,Nikos Chrisochoides,Brandon Weissbourd,Dushan N. Wadduwage*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIPPLE是一种通过稀疏校正实现轨迹注释的交互式工具，在多种显微镜视频数据集上以3-25倍更少的手动点击达到了与穷举标注相当的质量，填补了手动标注与全自动追踪之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微镜视频中非常规生物系统的动态追踪依赖专家标注数据，但穷举式手动标注难以规模化，缺少高效的半自动标注方法。

Method: 提出RIPPLE（精细插值平台用于点位置估计），用户点击起点后系统提出完整轨迹，用户仅需在轨迹偏离时进行稀疏校正干预。

Result: 在五个挑战性显微镜数据集（含透明水母Clytia hemisphaerica和快速运动精子）上测试，RIPPLE达到与穷举手动标注相当的质量，同时将手动点击次数减少3到25倍。

Conclusion: RIPPLE填补了手动标注与全自动追踪之间的缺失层，可即时量化生物动态、支持方法基准测试，并生成未来自动化追踪器所需的金标准数据。

Abstract: Tracking the dynamics of non-canonical biological systems in microscopy videos remains a persistent challenge. Both classical and learning-based trackers depend on expert-reviewed data to be evaluated and adapted, yet exhaustive manual annotation rarely scales to the videos where these tools are needed most. We developed RIPPLE (Refinement Interpolation Platform for Point Location Estimation), which recasts annotation as sparse correction: a user clicks a starting point, RIPPLE proposes a full trajectory, and the user intervenes only where the trajectory drifts. We tested RIPPLE on five challenging microscopy datasets from our laboratories, four from the transparent jellyfish Clytia hemisphaerica and one tracking landmarks on rapidly moving sperm. Across these, RIPPLE matched the quality of exhaustive manual annotation while reducing manual clicks by 3 to 25 times across datasets. RIPPLE thereby fills a missing layer between manual annotation and fully automated tracking, enabling immediate quantification of biological dynamics, method benchmarking, and the production of the gold-standard data needed to adapt future automated microscopy trackers.

</details>


### [12] [An Approach for Thyroid Nodule Analysis Using Thermographic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29221)
*J. R. González,É. O. Rodrigues,C. P. Damião,C. A. P. Fontes,A. C. Silva,A. C. Paiva,H. Li,C. Du,A. Conci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了甲状腺热成像技术，提出了图像采集协议、自主配准方法以及特征提取和分类流程，旨在通过热成像辅助甲状腺癌的早期检测，并作为大学医院预防性医疗行动的试点项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺癌发病率预计在未来十年显著上升，早期检测可提高生存率。传统医学诊断信息量大，医生难以在短时间内全面分析。热成像作为一种非侵入性诊断工具，有潜力辅助甲状腺异常检测，因此需要探索标准化的热成像分析流程。

Method: 论文采用综述方式聚焦甲状腺热成像分析，提出了图像采集协议、自主图像配准方法，并进行了包括特征提取、图像处理在内的数据分析，同时探索了区分健康与非健康患者的分类方法。

Result: 研究呈现了一个大学医院内用于肿瘤检测的试点项目，该项目是内分泌科预防性医疗行动的一部分。具体实验结果未在摘要中详细报告。

Conclusion: 该工作为热成像辅助甲状腺结节诊断提供了初步框架，未来经伦理委员会批准后将正式开展临床研究，以评估热成像在甲状腺结节诊断中的重要性。

Abstract: Thyroid cancer is said to be the second most common type of cancer in female individuals and the third in males by 2030, according to projections. In general, detecting cancer in its early stages improves the chance of survival of the individual. Thermography is a diagnostic tool that has been increasingly used to detect cancer and abnormalities, including that of thyroid. Various methods to segment and detect hot regions in thermograms and, consequently, to detect suspicious tissues present in these images have been proposed. It is well known that medical diagnosis yields a great deal of information. Thus, physicians have to comprehensively analyse and evaluate this information in a short period of time, which is infeasible in most cases. In this work, we perform a general review of thermography , focusing on the thyroid analysis. We propose protocols for image acquisiton and an autonomous registration for thyroid images. We also perform analyses of the image data, which include feature extraction, image processing, and a possible approach for classification of healthy or unhealthy patients. In summary, this work presents a pilot project for detection of tumors in our university hospital, which is part of an effort to support preventive medical actions in our endocrinology department. Under some future adjustments, this project will be submitted for approval by the ethics and research committee of Hospital Universitário Antonio Pedro at Universidade Federal Fluminense (HUAP-UFF) and to the Brazilian Ministry of Health Ethical committee under the name: Evaluation of the importance of thermography to aid diagnosis of thyroid nodules of patients in HUAP-UFF (in Portuguese: Avaliação da importância da termografia no auxílio à investigação diagnóstica de nódulos tireoidianos em pacientes acompanhados no HUAP-UFF).

</details>


### [13] [Toward Ethical Facial Age Estimation: A Generalized Zero-Shot Benchmark Without Training on Children's Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29230)
*Caio Petrucci,Leo Sampaio Ferraz Ribeiro,Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向面部年龄估计的广义零样本基准，训练数据明确排除儿童图像，但在零样本设置下评估模型对未成年人（<18岁）的泛化能力。实验表明，九种SOTA方法在该协议下性能大幅下降（平均46.4%，最高52.8%），且存在所见类别偏差，揭示了当前建模实践与现实伦理约束之间的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部年龄估计通常依赖包含未成年人图像的数据进行训练，这引发了严重的伦理、法律和隐私问题。现有方法缺乏在不使用儿童数据的前提下评估模型对年轻人群泛化能力的标准化基准，需要一个既能尊重数据使用约束又能评估模型分布外泛化能力的评估协议。

Method: 1) 重新审视六个广泛使用的数据集，提出标准化分割：训练/验证/测试集仅包含18-59岁样本，<18岁样本专用于零样本评估，60+样本作为分布偏移下的不可见验证集；2) 对含身份标注的数据集采用主体排他性分割以防止身份泄漏；3) 在该协议下评估九种SOTA年龄估计方法，分析其泛化失败模式及所见类别偏差。

Result: 所有评估方法在未见过的年龄组上均无法泛化，性能大幅下降（相对监督基线平均46.4%，最高52.8%）。模型并非简单退化，而是系统性地将未见年龄的预测锚定到邻近的已见类别上，表现出广义零样本学习中典型的所见类别偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作将无儿童数据的年龄估计形式化为现有数据集上的广义零样本基准，突显了当前建模实践与现实伦理约束之间的关键差距。所提出的基准为受限数据场景下的模型评估提供了原则性基础，鼓励开发对分布偏移鲁棒且与负责任数据使用一致的方法。

Abstract: Age estimation from facial images typically relies on training data that includes images of minors, a practice that raises serious ethical, legal, and privacy concerns. In this work, we propose a generalized zero-shot benchmark for facial age estimation that explicitly excludes children's data during training while still assessing model performance on younger populations. We revisit six widely used datasets and introduce standardized splits with strict age-group separation: samples aged 18-59 for training, validation, and testing; samples under 18 reserved exclusively for zero-shot evaluation; and samples 60+ as an unseen validation set for model selection under distribution shift. For datasets with identity annotations, subject-exclusive splits prevent identity leakage and better reflect real-world deployment conditions. Evaluating nine state-of-the-art age estimation methods under this protocol reveals that all evaluated methods consistently fail to generalize to unseen age groups, suffering substantial performance degradation -- on average 46.4%, and up to 52.8% -- relative to the supervised baseline. Moreover, models do not simply degrade: they systematically anchor predictions for unseen ages to nearby seen classes, a manifestation of the well-known seen-class bias in generalized zero-shot learning. By formalizing age estimation without children's data as a generalized zero-shot benchmark on existing datasets, this work highlights a critical gap between current modeling practices and real-world ethical constraints. Our benchmark provides a principled basis for evaluating models under restricted data regimes and encourages the development of methods that are robust to distribution shift and aligned with responsible data use.

</details>


### [14] [Deep Psychovisual Image Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29260)
*Wendi Ma,Aryaman Sharma,Wei Dai,Shekhar S. Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度视觉编码（Deep Visual Coding），一种受1990年代图像编码和复值表示启发的频域学习方法，将心理视觉抽象引入深度学习框架，实现更可解释和高效的视觉模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习视觉模型依赖同质化的空间层堆叠进行特征提取与聚合，导致决策过程不透明；而心理视觉模型表明人类视觉先形成中间抽象再进入高层认知，但目前缺乏将这种机制融入深度学习的方法。

Method: 提出Deep Visual Coding框架，采用数据驱动的频谱滤波器学习编码不同频带中的任务相关语义结构，结合复值图像表示实现心理视觉风格的抽象，构建首个基于心理视觉的深度学习模型。

Result: 心理视觉模型提取出高度可解释的目标部件，而普通CNN产生的是模糊区域；该模型对深度依赖更低，复值表示和学习抽象取代了深层空间层的功能。

Conclusion: 心理视觉编码为构建更高效、更透明的视觉模型提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Psychovisual models suggest human vision decouples low-level feature extraction from higher cognition by first forming intermediate abstractions. In contrast, deep learning-based vision models routinely extract and aggregate features using homogeneous stacks of spatial layers, rendering their decision-making processes opaque. In this paper, we propose Deep Visual Coding, a learned frequency-domain representation inspired by 1990s image codes that quantised perceptually salient frequencies, which together with complex-valued image representations produces psychovisual-style abstractions. This approach enables the first psychovisual-based deep learning framework, utilizing data-driven spectral filters that learn to encode task-relevant semantic structures within distinct frequency sub-bands. Salience analyses reveal that our psychovisual models extract highly interpretable object parts compared to the amorphous regions produced by regular Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). Furthermore, we find that our models are less depth dependent than CNNs for model scaling, since our complex-valued representations and learned abstractions subsume the role of the deep spatial layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that psychovisual coding provides a promising path toward more efficient and transparent vision models.

</details>


### [15] [Turbulence-Robust Dynamic Object Segmentation with Multi-Signal Priors and SAM2 Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29292)
*Bolian Peng,Ying Tang,Xu Liu,Long Sun,Xiaoqiang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的多信号分割流程，用于CVPR 2026 UG2+ Challenge Track 3中的动态目标分割任务，结合了预训练的运动估计、自监督语义先验、背景异常建模、手动校准的提议融合和SAM2掩码细化，在官方排行榜上达到了0.425041 mIoU和0.457206 mDice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气湍流会产生伪运动、模糊和间歇性目标可见性，使得单一运动线索不可靠，因此需要一种无需端到端训练、仅在推理模式下运行的多信号分割方案来应对这一挑战。

Method: 采用训练-free的多信号分割流程：RAFT用于密集运动响应，DINOv2用于语义目标先验，ViBe用于无训练背景建模，手动校准的提议融合，以及SAM2用于基于框提示的掩码细化。系统完全在推理模式下运行，不进行任何任务特定的模型训练或微调。

Result: 官方排行榜评估结果为0.425041 mIoU和0.457206 mDice。

Conclusion: 该训练-free方法适用于湍流环境下的动态目标分割，但更强的时序关联学习、自适应提议选择或任务特定适应可能进一步提升系统性能。

Abstract: This technical report presents our solution for the CVPR 2026 UG2+ Challenge Track 3: Dynamic Object Segmentation in Turbulence (DOST). We design a training-free multi-signal segmentation pipeline that combines pretrained motion estimation, self-supervised semantic priors, background anomaly modeling, manually calibrated proposal fusion, and SAM2-based mask refinement. The method uses RAFT for dense motion responses, DINOv2 for semantic objectness priors, ViBe for training-free background modeling, and pretrained SAM2 for box-prompt mask refinement.
  Instead of optimizing an end-to-end segmentation network, our system operates entirely in inference mode. This design is suitable for the DOST setting, where severe atmospheric turbulence produces pseudo-motion, blur, and intermittent target visibility, making a single motion cue unreliable. The final submitted masks are evaluated by the official leaderboard, which reports 0.425041 mIoU and 0.457206 mDice. Since no task-specific model training or fine-tuning is performed, stronger learned temporal association, adaptive proposal selection, or task-specific adaptation may further improve the system.

</details>


### [16] [Pocket-Dentist: On-Device Dental Image Understanding via Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29299)
*Kai Bian,Xucheng Guo,Bin Chen,Lingyan Ruan,Yiran Shen,Ting Dang,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pocket-Dentist，一个面向牙科多模态问答的效率感知基准，涵盖3个数据集、约1159名患者、5种任务类型和7项指标。实验发现紧凑型VLM（2B参数）在牙科图像理解中准确率更高且计算成本更低，微调后的Pocket-Dentist-2B在iPhone 17 Pro上每样本仅需4.31秒，延迟降低4.9倍，内存使用降低2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有牙科视觉语言模型评估分散在不同数据集、任务定义和指标上，且常忽略计算成本，限制了其在非专科中心的实际部署。

Method: 构建Pocket-Dentist基准，集成3个数据集、5种任务类型和7项指标；对14种VLM进行系统评估；微调紧凑型VLM（2B参数）并在iPhone 17 Pro上本地部署测试。

Result: 紧凑型VLM（2B参数）在准确率上优于大型VLM，计算成本显著更低。Pocket-Dentist-2B在iPhone 17 Pro上每样本4.31秒，延迟降低4.9倍，内存降低2.3倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑型VLM在牙科图像理解任务中兼具高准确率和低计算成本优势，适合本地部署，可实现隐私保护的临床预筛查。

Abstract: Evaluations of dental vision-language models remain fragmented across datasets, task definitions and metrics, and often ignore their computational cost. This limits their widespread deployment for dental screening outside specialist centres, where timely inference, limited hardware, and local handling of patient images are vital for practical, privacy-preserving clinical prescreening. Here we present Pocket-Dentist, an efficiency-aware benchmark for dental multimodal question answering that brings together three datasets spanning approximately 1,159 patients, five task types and seven metrics. Across typical 14 VLMs, our results reveals an interesting observation: compact VLMs (e.g., 2B-parameter models) outperform larger VLMs in accuracy while requiring substantially lower computational costs in dental image understanding. Deployed locally on an iPhone 17 Pro, our finetuned compact VLM Pocket-Dentist-2B processed each sample in 4.31 s, reducing latency by 4.9-fold and memory use by 2.3-fold compared with a 7B baseline.

</details>


### [17] [CapTalk: Text-Guided Stylization and Speech-Driven 3D Head Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29316)
*Xuangeng Chu,Yuan Gan,Ziteng Cui,Shuhong Liu,Jian Wang,Bing Zhou,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于音频驱动的3D面部动画生成框架，通过引入文本描述实现风格与情感的独立控制，支持动态情感变化和实时生成，解决了现有方法中风格固定、无法适配音频情感内容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动3D面部动画方法虽然能生成同步的唇部运动，但通常依赖预定义的身份或风格潜在特征，限制了用户自由控制说话风格的能力；且对整个音频段应用固定风格或身份会导致面部动画风格无法适配音频的情感内容。

Method: 重新审视风格与情感之间的纠缠关系；构建包含风格和情感文本描述的大规模数据集；提出一种新的说话头生成框架，以说话风格文本描述、角色情感文本描述和驱动音频流为输入，实现实时生成；支持推理过程中的动态情感控制。

Result: 生成结果在唇部运动同步性和面部表情表现力上均有提升，能够根据文本描述匹配相应的风格和情感，并支持音频中目标情感变化时的动态调整。

Conclusion: 通过分离风格与情感控制，该方法在保持高质量同步唇部运动的同时，赋予用户灵活的风格控制能力，并能动态适应音频中的情感变化，提升了生成动画的真实感和表现力。

Abstract: Audio-driven 3D facial animation aims to generate synchronized lip movements and vivid facial expressions from arbitrary audio clips. While existing methods can produce synchronized lip motions, they often rely on predefined identity or style latent features, which limits users' ability to freely control speaking styles. Moreover, applying a fixed style or identity to an entire audio segment typically results in facial animation styles that do not adapt to the emotional content of the audio. To address these challenges, we revisit the entanglement between style and emotion, construct a large-scale dataset with textual descriptions of both style and emotion, and propose a novel talking head generation framework that enables separate control over style and emotion. Our model takes as input both textual descriptions of speaking style and character emotion, as well as the driving audio stream, enabling real-time generation of highly synchronized lip movements and facial expressions that match the provided descriptions. Furthermore, our model supports dynamic emotion control during inference, allowing it to handle scenarios where the target emotion changes throughout the speech.

</details>


### [18] [Multi-Stage VLM Pipeline for Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29325)
*Fumiya Tatematsu,Fumihiko Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ACCIDENT挑战赛的冠军方案，基于冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct和235B MoE模型构建三阶段流水线（全视频联合预测、时间精炼、单帧定位），以9:1权重融合两模型输出并将预测点吸附到最近车辆检测上，最终在Public LB达到0.55469、Private LB达到0.57080，比最强基线（Molmo-7B, 0.358）高出约0.21，赢得比赛冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从CCTV监控视频中零样本预测事故时刻、碰撞质心和碰撞类型这一具有挑战性的问题，目标是在不额外训练的情况下利用预训练视觉语言模型的强大能力。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 全视频联合预测事故时刻与碰撞类型；(2) 时间精炼细化事故时刻；(3) 单帧定位碰撞质心。在冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct上执行该流水线，再在235B MoE模型上重复一次，以9:1权重融合两个输出，最后将每个预测点吸附到最近的车辆检测结果上。

Result: Public LB得分0.55469，Private LB得分0.57080，比最强基线Molmo-7B（0.358）高出约0.21，获得比赛冠军。

Conclusion: 该方案通过多阶段流水线设计、大模型集成和检测后处理，在零样本事故预测任务上取得了显著优于基线的结果。消融实验和负面结果报告为后续研究提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: We present the 1st-place solution to the ACCIDENT challenge at the CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop, which asks for zero-shot prediction of accident timing, impact centroid, and collision type from CCTV footage. On a frozen Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct checkpoint we build a three-stage pipeline (full-video joint prediction, time refinement, and single-frame grounding of the impact centroid), run the same pipeline a second time on a 235B Mixture-of-Experts sibling, blend the two outputs 9:1, and finally snap each predicted point onto the nearest vehicle detection. The final system reaches Public LB 0.55469 / Private LB 0.57080, roughly +0.21 over the strongest host baseline (Molmo-7B, 0.358) and wins the challenge. We ablate each component, report the negative results that shaped the final design, and release the code at https://github.com/fuumin621/cvpr2026-accident-1st-place-solution.

</details>


### [19] [EarthShift: a benchmark for measuring robustness to real-world distribution shifts in Earth observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29330)
*Kelsey Doerksen,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EarthShift是首个用于评估遥感领域地理空间基础模型在多种分布偏移（时间、地理、尺度、传感器等）下鲁棒性的公开测试平台。实验表明，8个模型在11个任务中，分布外性能平均下降15-20%，且鲁棒性与通用视觉模型及全监督模型相当，提示未来研究需关注分布鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测基准主要关注分布内泛化性能，但模型部署时面临多种分布偏移（如新时间段、地理位置、尺度、传感器），缺乏系统评估模型在真实分布偏移下鲁棒性的基准。

Method: 构建EarthShift测试平台，利用来自不同数据源、时间窗口、地理位置和传感器的成对数据集，通过比较分布内和分布外的性能来度量分布鲁棒性。在8个地理空间基础模型和11个任务上，覆盖5种偏移类型进行实验。

Result: 地理空间基础模型在分布外场景下平均性能下降15-20%，无论模型架构、规模、预训练或微调策略如何；其鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型及全监督模型相当。

Conclusion: 未来研究应致力于提升分布鲁棒性而不仅仅是模型性能，EarthShift可作为评估和指导鲁棒可靠基础模型研发的基准平台。

Abstract: Current Earth observation benchmarks focus on measuring performance on diverse tasks and applications, typically measuring generalization in-distribution. But when models are deployed, they must generalize to myriad out-of-distribution scenarios, such as new time periods, geographies, scales, and sensors. We introduce EarthShift: the first public testbed for benchmarking robustness across multiple realistic distribution shifts encountered in remote sensing. EarthShift enables users to measure distributional robustness by comparing performance in- and out-of-distribution using datasets from paired datasets from different sources, temporal windows, geographic locations, and sensors. Our experiments on 8 geospatial foundation models (GFMs) and 11 tasks covering 5 shift types show that GFMs consistently perform 15-20% worse out-of-distribution on average regardless of model architecture, size, pre-training or fine-tuning strategy. We show that GFM robustness is similar to that of generic vision foundation models, and even fully-supervised models. This highlights a need for future research to strive for improvements in distributional robustness, not just performance, which can be benchmarked using EarthShift. We release our code and datasets to provide a testbed to guide future work to create foundation models that are robust and reliable in real-world applications. Code and data for EarthShift are available at: https://earthshift.github.io

</details>


### [20] [Rethinking FID Through the Geometry of the Reference Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29335)
*Yunghee Lee,Byeonghyun Pak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了FID评估图像生成器时与样本质量不匹配的问题，发现参考数据集的几何特性（密度和有效秩）显著影响FID趋势：集中数据集下FID更可靠，而分散数据集下FID可能恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID被广泛使用但其分数降低并不总是对应样本质量提升，作者试图探究这种不匹配的原因，特别是参考数据集几何特性的影响。

Method: 在六个数据集上开展控制研究，分析分布密度和有效秩对FID随样本质量变化趋势的影响；通过精确率/召回率归因分析以及替换特征空间和距离度量进行消融实验。

Result: 数据集集中时FID趋势更有利（FID降低对应质量提升），而数据集分散时FID可能随样本质量改善反而变差；分布密度和有效秩是显著解释变量。

Conclusion: 分布度量应结合参考数据集的几何特性进行解读，以实现更可靠的基准评测。

Abstract: Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate image generators, yet lower FID does not always correspond to better sample quality. We show that this mismatch depends in part on the geometry of the reference dataset. In a controlled study across six datasets, distributional density and effective rank significantly explain how FID changes as sample quality improves. Concentrated datasets tend to yield more favorable FID trends, whereas more dispersed datasets can make FID worsen despite better samples. Attribution to precision and recall and ablations with alternative feature spaces and distances support the same conclusion. These results suggest that distributional metrics should be interpreted together with the geometry of the reference dataset for more reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [21] [DMC-CF: Dynamic Multimodal CounterFactual QA benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29339)
*Junzhe Zhang,Huixuan Zhang,Guirong Wang,Xingyao Zhang,Pei Liu,Lin Qu,Hu Wei,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模真实世界视频多模态因果反事实推理基准DMC-CF-Static，并提出动态图干预框架DGI，进而构建动态评估基准DMC-CF-Dynamic。实验表明，当前多模态大模型在真实场景下的因果推理能力仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推理数据集规模有限，且多基于合成图像/视频、卡通或非真实多模态数据，缺乏基于真实世界视频的大规模基准，无法有效评估MLLMs在真实场景下的因果推理能力。

Method: 收集真实世界视频构建DMC-CF-Static静态基准；提出动态图干预（DGI）框架，利用因果图表示因果事件，从静态基准构建DMC-CF-Dynamic动态评估基准，缓解数据污染问题。

Result: 在综合的DMC-CF（含静态和动态基准）上的实验结果表明，当前多模态大语言模型在多模态因果推理，特别是真实世界场景下的能力仍显不足。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs在真实世界多模态因果反事实推理任务上表现欠佳，亟需进一步改进；DMC-CF基准可有效评估和推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), models have demonstrated increasingly powerful multimodal capabilities. However, whether MLLMs trained through statistical learning can truly understand the causal relationships underlying the real world remains a key research question. In recent years, numerous multimodal causal reasoning datasets have been proposed. Nevertheless, these datasets are either limited in scale or constructed from synthetic images and videos, cartoon-based content, or other non-realistic multimodal sources. To address these limitations, we collect real-world videos and construct DMC-CF-Static, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal causal counterfactual reasoning. Furthermore, to mitigate issues such as data contamination in traditional static evaluation, we represent causal events using causal graphs and propose the Dynamic Graph Intervention (DGI) framework to build the dynamic evaluation benchmark DMC-CF-Dynamic from DMC-CF-Static. Experimental results on the overall DMC-CF, which includes both static and dynamic evaluation benchmarks, demonstrate that the multimodal causal reasoning capabilities of current multimodal large language models in real-world scenarios still require substantial improvement.

</details>


### [22] [WorldMemArena: Evaluating Multimodal Agent Memory Through Action-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29341)
*Chengzhi Liu,Yuzhe Yang,Sophia Xiao Pu,Yepeng Liu,Lin Long,Yichen Guo,Nuo Chen,Zhaotian Weng,Elena Kochkina,Simerjot Kaur,Charese Smiley,Xiaomo Liu,James Zou,Sheng Liu,Yuheng Bu,Songyou Peng,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldMemArena，一个400个多会话多模态任务的基准，用于诊断智能体记忆系统在写入、维护、检索和使用四个阶段的失败原因。实验发现：更好的记忆存储不保证更好性能；多模态记忆难以充分利用视觉证据；系统跨领域不稳定；基于框架的记忆更灵活但成本高且可靠性低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只评测静态对话中的回忆能力，将记忆简化为单一任务准确率，视觉观察被降级为文字描述，无法定位失败具体发生在写入、维护、检索还是使用阶段。随着智能体框架自主管理记忆的兴起，亟需可比较手工设计与自主方案的评估方法。

Method: 将多模态智能体记忆形式化为行动-世界交互循环的四阶段生命周期（写入、维护、检索、使用），构建WorldMemArena基准：400个多会话任务，涵盖终身演化（个人与任务状态演变）和智能体执行（来自真实观察、行动和反馈的记忆），并标注了黄金记忆点、更新、干扰物和证据链。

Result: (1)更好的记忆写入和存储不保证更好的性能；(2)多模态记忆系统仍难以充分利用视觉证据；(3)系统跨领域不稳定且在真实智能体轨迹上性能退化；(4)框架记忆更灵活但成本更高且可靠性更低。

Conclusion: 现有记忆系统在多模态长周期智能体任务中存在显著缺陷，需要针对四阶段生命周期更精细的评估与改进，特别是多模态证据利用和跨领域稳定性方面。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are increasingly deployed as long-horizon agents, where memory must do more than recall: it must track an evolving world, revise what has gone stale, and surface the right evidence at decision time. Existing benchmarks measure recall over static dialogue, collapse memory into a single end-of-task accuracy, and reduce visual observations to captions, leaving us unable to localize failures to writing, maintenance, retrieval, or use. The rise of agent harnesses that author their own memory sharpens this gap, since we have no principled way to compare hand-designed pipelines with self-managing alternatives. To close these gaps, we formulate multimodal agent memory as an Action-World Interaction Loop with an observable four-stage lifecycle, and instantiate it in WorldMemArena: 400 multi-session multimodal tasks spanning Lifelong Evolution (evolving personal and task states) and Agentic Execution (memory from real observations, actions, and feedback), annotated with gold memory points, updates, distractors, and evidence chains for stage-level diagnosis. This enables the first head-to-head comparison of long-context, manually designed (RAG and external memory systems), and harness-based memory agents. Results show that: (1) better memory writing and storage do not guarantee better performance; (2) multimodal memory still struggles to fully use visual evidence; (3) systems are unstable across domains and degrade on realistic agentic trajectories; and (4) harness memory is more flexible but remains costly and less reliable.

</details>


### [23] [Orthogonal Negative Guidance in Attention Feature Space for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29390)
*Jungmin Ko,Jungwon Park,Jimyeong Kim,Changin Choi,Wonseok Lee,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力特征空间的正交负引导方法（Orthogonal Negative Guidance），在无需训练的情况下，通过正交化负提示注意力特征与正提示特征，仅减去正交分量，实现对指定概念的有效抑制，同时保持图像质量和语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型虽能生成高质量图像，但在显式消除指定物体或属性方面存在困难。现有方法如提示否定、后处理编辑和负引导等，要么无法有效移除目标概念，要么降低整体图像质量，缺乏对概念抑制的显式控制。

Method: 提出Orthogonal Negative Guidance方法，在MM-DiT架构的注意力输出空间中，将负提示的注意力特征相对于正提示特征进行正交化，仅减去正交分量，从而抑制不想要的语义同时保留期望语义。该方法无需训练，支持多概念抑制和可调抑制强度。

Result: 在FLUX-dev和FLUX-schnell上的实验表明，该方法在概念抑制、提示对齐和图像质量之间取得了优越的平衡。人工评估中，该方法比第二好的基线高出18.78%。

Conclusion: 提出的正交负引导方法有效解决了T2I模型中的显式概念抑制问题，无需额外训练即可实现可控的、高质量的概念去除，并具有多概念和可调抑制的扩展能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have become increasingly capable of generating high-quality images. Yet, enforcing the explicit absence of a specified object or attribute remains a fundamentally challenging problem. Existing approaches, including prompt negation, post-hoc editing, and negative guidance, remain insufficient for explicit concept suppression, often failing to remove the target concept or degrading overall image quality. To this end, we propose Orthogonal Negative Guidance in attention feature space, a training-free method that operates in the attention output space of MM-DiT-based T2I transformers. Our method orthogonalizes negative-prompt attention features with respect to positive-prompt features and subtracts only the orthogonal component, suppressing unwanted concepts while preserving desired semantics. Experiments on FLUX-dev and FLUX-schnell show that our method achieves favorable trade-offs between concept suppression, prompt alignment, and image quality. In human evaluation, our method outperforms the second-best baseline by 18.78%. We further show that our method supports multi-concept suppression and adjustable concept suppression.

</details>


### [24] [Semantic and Visual Evidence for Efficient Long-Video Reasoning: A Solution for the HD-EPIC VQA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29402)
*Yinsong Xu,Wei Jing,Liuxin Zhang,Wanjun Lv,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为语义证据和视觉证据，通过粗到细的提取管道获取全局程序结构，利用边界框和视觉嵌入保留细粒度视觉细节，在查询时动态检索并整合两种证据，在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战中取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在处理长距离自我中心视频时存在上下文长度有限和细粒度视觉细节定位不足的问题，现有模型在HD-EPIC基准上表现较低。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为：(1) 语义证据——通过粗到细提取管道捕获全局程序结构；(2) 视觉证据——通过边界框和视觉嵌入保留对象级别的细粒度定位。推理时将问题作为查询条件，动态检索并整合两种来源的证据。

Result: 在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战的多个任务类别上取得了竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 显式地对语义证据和视觉证据进行结构化、检索和整合，对于利用多模态大模型实现有效的长视频理解至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding long-form egocentric videos remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to limited context length and insufficient grounding of fine-grained visual details. The recently proposed HD-EPIC benchmark highlights these limitations: even strong long-context models achieve relatively low performance across diverse video question answering tasks. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that decouples long-video reasoning into two complementary forms of evidence: semantic evidence and visual evidence. Semantic evidence captures global procedural structure through a coarse-to-fine extraction pipeline, while object-centric visual evidence preserves fine-grained grounding through bounding boxes and visual embeddings. During inference, we formulate reasoning as a query-conditioned evidence retrieval and integration process, dynamically selecting relevant information from both sources. Our approach achieves competitive performance in the HD-EPIC-VQA Challenge across multiple task categories. More broadly, our results demonstrate that explicitly structuring, retrieving, and integrating semantic and visual evidence is critical for effective long-video understanding with MLLMs.

</details>


### [25] [One Click per Cell Type Suffices: Training-free Group Interaction for Cell Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29429)
*Sanghyun Jo,Seo Jin Lee,Seohyung Hong,Yoorim Gang,Hyeongsub Kim,Hyungseok Seo,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Chain-of-Prompts (CoP)的训练无关框架，通过Group Prompting范式将对细胞实例分割的交互从每实例O(N)降至每类型O(T)，利用SAM编码器特征空间中同类细胞的自然聚类特性，仅需每类型一次点击即可分割所有实例，在多个基准上保留超过90%的逐实例性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的细胞实例分割模型在跨域细胞类型上性能严重下降；交互式基础模型虽能解决但需要逐实例提示，在高密度组织病理学图像中成本过高（O(N)）。目标是降低交互成本同时保持分割质量。

Method: 提出Group Prompting新范式及CoP框架：利用SAM冻结图像编码器特征空间中同类细胞自动聚类的特性，通过(1)非参数化门控机制筛选多尺度编码器特征中的可靠同类型位置，(2)选择空间最远的可靠点作为下一个提示以最大化覆盖，递归扩展单次用户点击。

Result: 在三个细胞类型标注基准上，每类型一次点击保留超过90%的逐实例全提示性能；在四个形态同质基准上，单次点击保留超过99%性能；无需任何额外训练即可超越完全监督方法。

Conclusion: CoP证明无需训练即可有效利用SAM的隐式特征聚类性质实现高效的组级交互式分割，为高密度组织病理学图像中的细胞分割提供了一种实用且经济的方案。

Abstract: Cell instance segmentation models trained on cell-specific datasets suffer severe performance drops on out-of-distribution cell types, while interactive foundation models overcome this through per-instance prompting at a cost that is prohibitively expensive for histopathology images containing hundreds to thousands of densely packed instances. We introduce Group Prompting, a new paradigm that shifts interactive segmentation from per-instance $O(N)$ to per-type $O(T)$, where a single click per cell type suffices to segment all instances of that type. Our key observation is that the frozen image encoder of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) already clusters same-type cells in its feature space before any prompt is given. Exploiting this property, we propose Chain-of-Prompts (CoP), a training-free framework that recursively expands a single user click by (1) identifying reliable same-type locations through non-parametric gating of multi-scale encoder features, and (2) selecting the most spatially distant reliable point as the next prompt to maximize coverage. On three cell-type-annotated benchmarks, CoP with one click per type retains over 90% of per-instance performance and surpasses fully-supervised methods without any additional training. On four morphologically homogeneous benchmarks, a single click retains over 99%. Project Page: https://shjo-april.github.io/Chain-of-Prompts/

</details>


### [26] [Recovering Policy-Induced Errors: Benchmarking and Trajectory Synthesis for Robust GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29447)
*Tianpeng Bu,Xin Liu,Qihua Chen,Hao Jiang,Shurui Li,Hongtao Duan,Lu Jiang,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GUI-RobustEval评估基准和Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis (RoTS)数据合成框架，通过800k高质量数据微调得到RoTS-7B/32B模型，显著提升了GUI智能体的错误恢复能力，RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到47.4%成功率，实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体缺乏从自身错误中恢复的鲁棒性，阻碍了实际部署。现有评估和训练数据均未系统覆盖错误恢复能力，因此需要在评估和数据层面同时弥补这一差距。

Method: 1) 提出GUI-RobustEval评估基准，包含1,216个可执行测试用例，系统测量多种错误模式的恢复能力；2) 提出RoTS可扩展合成框架，通过基于树的流水线主动发现多样化错误模式并合成对应恢复步骤，生成800k高质量数据；3) 在合成数据上微调得到RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B模型。

Result: RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B在GUI-RobustEval和传统GUI基准上均显著提升。RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到47.4%成功率和33.8% All-Pass@4分数，取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 改进的长程错误恢复能力不仅提升了鲁棒性，也促进了整体性能的提升。代码已开源。

Abstract: While GUI agents have advanced rapidly, they often lack the robustness to recover from their own errors, hindering real-world deployment. To bridge this gap at both the evaluation and data levels, we introduce GUI-RobustEval and propose Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis. GUI-RobustEval contains $1,216$ executable test cases that systematically measure error recovery capabilities across a broad and realistic spectrum of error modes. At the data level, RoTS is a scalable synthesis framework that creates $800k$ high-quality data via a tree-based pipeline that proactively discovers diverse error modes and synthesizes corresponding recovery steps. Our two models, RoTS-7B and RoTS-32B, fine-tuned on our dataset, both demonstrate significant gains on GUI-RobustEval and traditional GUI benchmarks. Notably, RoTS-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance on OSWorld, with a $47.4\%$ success rate and a $33.8\%$ All-Pass@4 score, suggesting that improved long-horizon error recovery ability contributes to both robustness and overall performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/RoTS.

</details>


### [27] [Comparative evaluation of photogrammetric reconstruction methods and 3D Gaussian Splatting for road surface roughness analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29452)
*Marouane Elmegdar,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)四种基于智能手机图像的三维重建管线，评估它们估算道路表面粗糙度的能力。结果表明COLMAP对微观纹理最敏感，Meshroom重建平衡，Metashape几何最平滑，3DGS能捕捉不规则但噪声大。开源管线可用于低成本路面监测的相对粗糙度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于传感器的道路表面评估技术成本高昂，而基于图像的三维重建提供了一种低成本替代方案。本研究旨在评估不同重建管线在路面粗糙度估算中的表现，为低成本路面监测提供实用方法。

Method: 使用四种重建管线（COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape、3DGS）从智能手机图像进行三维重建；所有点云在CloudCompare中经统一流程处理（方向对齐、分割、法线估计、粗糙度计算），并在0.2、0.4、0.6模型单位的邻域半径下计算粗糙度。

Result: COLMAP对微观纹理敏感性最高；Meshroom重建效果均衡，粗糙度变化适中；Metashape因内部滤波产生最平滑的几何结构；3DGS能捕获可见不规则性但噪声更高、密度更低。

Conclusion: 开源管线可用于相对粗糙度评估，为低成本路面监测提供了实用方法。

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction offers a low-cost alternative to traditional sensor-based techniques for road surface assessment. This study compares four reconstruction pipelines--COLMAP, Meshroom, Metashape, and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)--to evaluate their ability to estimate road surface roughness from smartphone imagery. All point clouds were processed in CloudCompare using a consistent workflow involving orientation alignment, segmentation, normal estimation, and roughness computation at neighborhood radiuses of 0.2, 0.4, and 0.6 model units. The results show that COLMAP provides the highest sensitivity to micro-texture, while Meshroom yields balanced reconstructions with moderate roughness variation. Metashape produces the smoothest geometry due to its internal filtering, and 3DGS captures visible irregularities but exhibits higher noise and lower density. The comparison demonstrates that open-source pipelines are viable for relative roughness evaluation, offering a practical approach for low-cost pavement monitoring.

</details>


### [28] [Uni-RCM: Unified Reference-guided Cross-modal Mapping for Multi-Class Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29455)
*Yangchen Wu,Huiqiang Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Uni-RCM框架，通过参考引导块（Reference Guide Block）和离线残差量化器（Offline Residual Quantizer）解决多类别工业异常检测中类间干扰和特征流形混淆问题，在MVTec-3D AD数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态工业异常检测通常为每个产品类别单独建模，缺乏可扩展性；统一范式下检测精度因类间干扰和特征流形混淆而下降。本文旨在解决这些问题，实现高效的多类别统一异常检测。

Method: 1）参考引导块（Reference Guide Block）：引入可学习参考特征动态过滤类别特定噪声，捕获跨模态共性；2）离线残差量化器（Offline Residual Quantizer）：通过多个级联码本表征正态分布。

Result: 在MVTec-3D AD数据集上，在图像级检测和像素级定位任务中均达到SOTA性能，尤其在挑战性的多类别设置下表现优异。

Conclusion: Uni-RCM框架有效解决了多类别统一异常检测中的类间干扰问题，验证了参考引导和残差量化策略在多模态工业异常检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-modal industrial anomaly detection typically relies on separate models for each product category, fundamentally limiting practical scalability. When shifting to a unified paradigm that handles diverse classes simultaneously, detection accuracy often degrades due to inter-class interference and feature manifold confusion. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Unified Reference guided Cross-modal Mapping framework, named Uni-RCM. At its core, we propose a reference guide block to dynamically filter out category-specific noise by introducing a learnable reference feature, which captures the commonalities across different modalities. Besides, an offline residual quantizer is proposed to characterize the normal distribution by multiple cascaded codebooks. Extensive evaluations on the MVTec-3D AD dataset demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance in the challenging multi-class setting and in terms of image-level detection and pixel-level localization.

</details>


### [29] [FedSmoothLoRA: Toward Smoother and Faster Convergence in Federated Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29460)
*Zehao Wang,Guanglei Yang,Yihan Zeng,Hang Xu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo,Chun-Mei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedSmoothLoRA 提出了一种联邦 LoRA 微调框架，通过 Round-Matching 矩阵和 Gradient-Aligned 矩阵分别解决跨轮次状态不匹配和客户端无关初始状态问题，同时保留扩展的更新空间，实现更平滑、更快速的收敛，在图像分类和自然语言生成任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接组合 FedAvg 和 LoRA 存在三个关键问题：1) 有限更新空间限制了模型有效学习能力；2) 跨轮次状态不匹配破坏了局部优化连续性；3) 客户端无关的初始状态降低了局部收敛速度。现有方法仅缓解了有限更新空间问题，但后两个问题未得到充分解决。

Method: FedSmoothLoRA 在每个通信轮次构建局部 LoRA 初始化，使用两个矩阵：Round-Matching 矩阵用于保持跨轮次的局部状态连续性，Gradient-Aligned 矩阵通过局部数据估计的梯度信号提供客户端特定的优化指导。

Result: 在图像分类和自然语言生成任务上的大量实验表明，FedSmoothLoRA 持续优于现有的联邦 LoRA 微调方法。

Conclusion: FedSmoothLoRA 通过 Round-Matching 矩阵和 Gradient-Aligned 矩阵的设计，同时解决了联邦 LoRA 微调中的三个关键问题，实现了更平滑、更快速的收敛，为联邦基础模型微调提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of foundation models with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides an efficient solution for reducing communication and computation costs while preserving data locality. However, the direct combination of FedAvg and LoRA suffers from three key issues: limited update space, which restricts the model's effective learning capacity; inter-round state mismatch, which disrupts cross-round local optimization continuity; and a client-agnostic starting state, which slows local convergence on clients. Although recent methods mitigate the limited update space issue by merging LoRA updates into the backbone across communication rounds, inter-round state mismatch and the client-agnostic starting state remain insufficiently addressed. To address these issues, we propose FedSmoothLoRA, a federated LoRA tuning framework that preserves the enlarged update space, improves cross-round local optimization continuity, and provides a client-aware starting state for local training. At each communication round, FedSmoothLoRA constructs the local LoRA initialization using two matrices: a Round-Matching matrix that preserves cross-round local state continuity, and a Gradient-Aligned matrix that provides client-specific optimization guidance from gradient signals estimated on local data. Together, these designs enable smoother and faster convergence. Extensive experiments on image classification and natural language generation tasks demonstrate that FedSmoothLoRA consistently outperforms existing federated LoRA tuning methods. Code: https://github.com/wangzehao0704/FedSmoothLoRA

</details>


### [30] [FlowSeg: Dynamic Semantic Guidance for LLM-Conditioned Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29461)
*Zekang Zhang,Guangyu Gao,Youyun Tang,ChengJing Wu,Xiaochao Qu,Chi Harold Liu,Jianbo Jiao,Yunchao Wei,Luoqi Liu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowSeg，通过引入双向语义流（语言条件与视觉特征的交互更新）解决现有分割方法中语言条件与生成掩码语义不对齐的问题，在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"先提议后选择"的分割流程中，语言语义通常仅作为静态提示或事后匹配信号，未参与迭代掩码生成过程，导致高质量掩码候选被生成后，最终预测仍可能与语言条件语义不对齐。

Method: FlowSeg提出双向语义流机制：(1) 语言条件在掩码生成的每个阶段主动引导掩码细化；(2) 条件嵌入随视觉证据的出现逐步更新。同时引入轻量级边界感知细化模块，选择性增强不确定区域而不扰动置信区域。

Result: 在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上，FlowSeg持续提升了语言-掩码对齐度，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过让语言条件参与迭代生成过程而非仅作为静态输入或事后匹配信号，能够有效解决语义不对齐问题，生成语义上有依据的掩码表示和视觉上对齐的语言条件。

Abstract: LLM-conditioned segmentation has recently advanced rapidly by coupling large language models with iterative mask generation frameworks. However, we identify a persistent failure mode in current propose-then-select pipelines. Although high-quality mask candidates are often generated, the final prediction may fail to match the given linguistic condition. This failure arises because language semantics are typically used as static prompts or post-hoc matching signals, rather than participating in the iterative mask generation process. Through systematic analysis, we show that many errors stem from semantic misalignment rather than poor mask quality. To address this issue, we propose FlowSeg, which introduces dynamic semantic guidance via a bidirectional semantic flow between intermediate decoding states and LLM-derived condition embeddings throughout the generation process. Language conditions actively guide mask refinement at each stage, while condition embeddings are progressively updated by emerging visual evidence. This design yields semantically grounded mask representations and visually aligned language conditions, enabling more reliable matching. We further incorporate a lightweight boundary-aware refinement to selectively enhance uncertain regions without perturbing confident interiors. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation and reasoning segmentation tasks demonstrate that FlowSeg consistently improves language-mask alignment and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://zkzhang98.github.io/FlowSeg_page

</details>


### [31] [Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on CFMME: A Comprehensive Chinese Financial Multimodal Evaluation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29462)
*Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CFMME，一个中文金融多模态评估基准，包含6,052个实例，覆盖8种金融图像模态和4种核心多模态任务。评估显示最佳模型在问答任务上准确率仅66.11%，在检测、识别和信息提取任务上平均得分77.18，表明当前LVLMs在金融领域仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM评估基准缺乏针对中文金融业务全流程的感知、理解、推理和认知能力的综合评估框架，无法全面衡量模型在真实金融场景中的表现。

Method: 构建CFMME基准，包含6,052个实例，覆盖8种主要金融图像模态（从基础学术知识到复杂实际应用）和4种核心多模态任务（问答、检测、识别、信息提取），并对代表性LVLM进行系统评估。

Result: 最优模型在问答任务上整体准确率为66.11%，在检测、识别和信息提取任务上平均得分为77.18。此外，对错误原因、跨模态能力和多方向设置进行了详细分析。

Conclusion: 当前LVLMs在金融领域多模态任务上仍存在显著不足，CFMME基准可为未来提升LVLM在金融领域的多模态性能提供有价值的参考和方向。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has substantially expanded model capabilities beyond text-only understanding, enabling unified inference across both visual and textual modalities and supporting a broader range of real-world applications. To comprehensively evaluate the perception, understanding, reasoning, and cognition capabilities of LVLMs throughout the entire financial business workflow in Chinese contexts, we introduce CFMME, a novel Chinese financial multimodal evaluation benchmark. CFMME comprises 6,052 instances spanning from fundamental academic knowledge to complex real-world applications, covering eight primary financial image modalities and four core multimodal tasks. On CFMME, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative LVLMs. The results show that the state-of-the-art model attains an overall accuracy of 66.11\% on the question answering task and an average score of 77.18 on the detection, recognition, and information extraction tasks, indicating substantial room for improvement in current LVLMs. In addition, we conduct detailed analyses of error causes, cross-modal capabilities, and multi-orientation settings, yielding valuable insights for future research. We hope that CFMME will spur further progress in LVLMs, especially by improving their performance on multiple multimodal tasks in the financial domain.

</details>


### [32] [V2XCrafter: Learning to Generate Driving Scene Across Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29471)
*Yihang Tao,Yu Guo,Senkang Hu,Yanan Ma,Zihan Fang,Sam Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出V2XCrafter，首个面向多智能体协作驾驶场景的跨视角可控街景生成框架，通过渐进式多智能体扩散模型和跨智能体注意力模块解决生成质量退化与跨车一致性问题，有效增强下游协作3D检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X协作驾驶系统受限于稀缺的标注数据和有限的泛化能力，而图像生成技术虽可用于数据增强，但面向单车多视角的现有方法在多智能体场景中面临两大挑战：学习目标扩展导致生成质量退化，以及跨智能体动态变化难以建模联合观测对象物理属性的一致性。

Method: 提出V2XCrafter框架，包含：(1) 渐进式多智能体扩散模型，基于单智能体主干，利用邻接智能体潜在状态作为参考信号逐步引导单到多扩散；(2) 跨智能体注意力模块，利用协作视图图和可学习的联合观测对象表示建模动态跨智能体相机视角关系。

Result: 实验证明V2XCrafter能够生成高保真、可控且跨智能体一致的街景视图，有效提升下游协作3D目标检测任务性能。

Conclusion: V2XCrafter作为首个多智能体协作驾驶场景可控生成框架，通过渐进式扩散与跨智能体一致性建模，成功解决了数据稀缺和泛化限制问题，为V2X协作感知数据增强提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Collaborative driving systems leverage vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication for multi-agent collaborative perception to enhance driving safety, yet they remain constrained by scarce annotated real-world V2X driving datasets and limited generalization across diverse driving conditions. While image generation technology offers a feasible solution for data augmentation, existing methods tailored for single-vehicle multi-view scenarios face two fundamental challenges in multi-agent driving settings: (1) the expansion of the learning objective degrades generation quality, and (2) the highly dynamic variations across agents hinder the modeling of consistency for physical attributes (e.g., color, category) in jointly observed objects. To bridge this gap, we propose V2XCrafter, the first framework for generating controllable and realistic collaborative driving scene across agents' camera views. For effective learning, we develop a progressive multi-agent diffusion model based on a single-agent backbone, using neighboring agents' latent states as reference signals to progressively guide the single-to-multi diffusion. To address cross-vehicle inconsistency, we propose a cross-agent attention module that leverages a collaboration view graph and learnable jointly observed object representation to model the dynamic cross-agent camera view relationships. Experiments have shown that V2XCrafter can generate high-fidelity and controllable street views with consistency across agents, thereby effectively enhancing the downstream collaborative 3D object detection tasks.

</details>


### [33] [AnyMo: Scaling Any-Modality Conditional Motion Generation with Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29488)
*Yiheng Li,Zhuo Li,Ruibing Hou,Yingjie Chen,Hong Chang,Hao Liu,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniHuMo大规模多模态对齐运动数据集（5000+小时、320万序列）和AnyMo统一框架，结合残差FSQ运动分词器与可扩展掩码建模Transformer，实现任意模态组合下的高质量人体运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件运动生成方法受限于固定模态配置和任务特定架构，跨模态交互和多模态条件合成尚未充分探索，且缺乏大规模模态对齐的运动数据。

Method: 构建OmniHuMo大规模多模态对齐数据集；提出AnyMo框架，包含残差FSQ运动分词器和可扩展掩码建模Transformer，支持任意模态组合的灵活控制。

Result: AnyMo在多种模态组合下实现高保真运动合成，支持空间和风格属性的灵活控制。

Conclusion: 大规模多模态对齐数据与统一框架可有效提升条件运动生成的泛化能力和合成质量，突破现有方法在固定模态配置上的局限。

Abstract: Conditional human motion generation remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and robotics. Despite significant progress, current methods are often constrained by fixed modality configurations and task-specific architectures, leaving cross-modal interactions and the scaling laws of multimodal-conditioned synthesis largely underexplored. A key bottleneck is the scarcity of large-scale modality-aligned motion data, limiting generalization across diverse control signals. In this work, we introduce OmniHuMo, a large-scale, high-quality dataset comprising over 5,000 hours of motion and 3.2 million sequences with precisely aligned multimodal annotations (e.g., text, speech, music, and trajectory). Leveraging OmniHuMo, we propose AnyMo, a unified multimodal framework combining a Residual FSQ-based motion tokenizer with a scalable masked modeling transformer, enabling high-quality motion synthesis under arbitrary modality combinations. Extensive experiments show that AnyMo achieves high-fidelity synthesis while offering flexible control over both spatial and stylistic attributes.

</details>


### [34] [KGEdit: Ambiguity-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Training-Free Precise Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29509)
*Mingshu Cai,Miao Zhang,Chenghe Yang,Yixuan Li,Osamu Yoshie,Yuya Ieiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KGEdit提出了一种基于知识图谱的结构化语义控制框架，通过构建消歧知识图谱（AAKG）、结构化语义注入模块（SSIM）和时序感知语义控制模块（TASC），解决了训练无关视频生成中的语义歧义、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致问题，在编辑精度和时间稳定性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关视频生成方法在处理复杂文本指令时存在语义歧义、错误概念绑定和跨帧不一致等问题，亟需一种更具结构化和可控性的语义控制方案。

Method: 1) 构建消歧知识图谱（AAKG）将输入提示解耦为身份、关系、属性和否定约束四类结构化语义；2) 设计结构化语义注入模块（SSIM）将语义信号注入扩散Transformer的关键层；3) 引入时序感知语义控制模块（TASC）根据去噪过程的阶段性特征动态调度语义目标。

Result: KGEdit在编辑精度和时间稳定性上优于现有方法，并在文本驱动交互场景中展现出更高的效率和可控性。

Conclusion: KGEdit通过结构化知识图谱和时序感知语义控制，有效提升了文本到视频生成的语义对齐精度和时间一致性，为复杂指令下的视频编辑提供了高效可控的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, training-free video generation has progressed remarkably. However, when handling complex textual instructions, existing methods still suffer from semantic ambiguity, incorrect concept binding, and cross-frame inconsistency. To address these issues, we propose KGEdit, a structured semantic control framework for text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models. Specifically, we first construct an ambiguity-aware knowledge graph (AAKG) to disentangle and disambiguate the input prompt, converting it into four types of structured semantics: identity, relation, attribute, and negative constraints. We then design a structured semantic injection module (SSIM) to inject these semantic signals into key layers of the diffusion Transformer, enabling fine-grained semantic control. In addition, we introduce a temporal-aware semantic control (TASC) module that dynamically schedules semantic objectives according to the stage-wise characteristics of the denoising process, further improving semantic alignment and temporal consistency. Experiments show that KGEdit outperforms existing methods in editing precision and temporal stability, while offering higher efficiency and controllability in text-driven interaction scenarios.

</details>


### [35] [GiPL: Generative augmented iterative Pseudo-Labeling for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29539)
*Jiacong Liu,Shu Luo,Yikai Qin,Yaze Zhao,Yongwei Jiang,Yixiong Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GiPL，一种双分支训练框架，通过迭代伪标签自训练和基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强，解决跨域小样本目标检测中支持集利用不足和过拟合问题，在三个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域小样本目标检测（CD-FSOD）中，视觉语言基础模型微调面临两个关键挑战：一是支持集标注稀疏（单实例标注）导致利用不充分，二是目标域样本极度有限导致严重过拟合。

Method: 提出GiPL双分支训练框架：(1) 第一分支采用迭代伪标签自训练范式，对支持集进行零样本推理生成可靠伪标注，与真实标签融合后迭代优化模型；(2) 第二分支引入基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强流水线，合成域对齐、多目标标注的图像以丰富训练样本并抑制过拟合。

Result: 在RUOD、CARPK、CarDD三个跨域小样本目标检测数据集上，在1/5/10-shot设置下，GiPL一致性地超越了现有最先进方法，且性能提升显著。

Conclusion: GiPL通过双分支设计有效解决了CD-FSOD中支持集利用不足和过拟合两大问题，验证了迭代伪标签自训练与生成式数据增强相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown promising zero-shot generalization for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD). However, they face two critical challenges in fine-tuning: insufficient support set utilization due to sparse single-instance annotations, and severe overfitting under extremely limited target-domain samples. To address these issues, this paper proposes GiPL, an efficient two-branch training framework.In the first branch, we design an iterative pseudo-label self-training paradigm, which performs zero-shot inference on the support set to generate reliable pseudo-annotations, fuses them with ground-truth labels, and iteratively optimizes the model to fully exploit support set data. In the second branch, we introduce generative data augmentation pipeline using large vision-language models, which synthesizes domain-aligned, multi-object annotated images to enrich training samples and suppress overfitting. Extensive experiments on three challenging CD-FSOD datasets (RUOD, CARPK, CarDD) under 1/5/10-shot settings demonstrate that GiPL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with significant performance gains.Code is available at \href{https://github.com/z-yaz/CDiscover}{CDiscover}.

</details>


### [36] [TAE: Target-aware enhancer for nighttime UAV tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29558)
*Yanyan Chen,Ruigang Fu,Yu Song,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAE（目标感知低光增强框架），通过跟踪边界框的弱监督信号实现区域感知增强，并采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制，解决夜间无人机跟踪中的图像退化问题。同时贡献了包含268个序列的DarkSOT夜间无人机跟踪基准数据集，实验证明TAE显著提升夜间低光场景下的跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间低光条件下图像严重退化是制约无人机单目标跟踪全天候应用的核心瓶颈，现有图像增强方法难以区分目标和背景区域，容易放大背景噪声或破坏目标特征。

Method: 提出TAE框架：(1) 利用跟踪边界框的弱监督信号进行显式引导，实现区域感知增强；(2) 采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制，对不同区域进行精细化建模和自适应调整。

Result: 在DarkSOT和UAVDark135数据集上，TAE显著提升了夜间低光场景下的跟踪性能，展现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: TAE框架有效解决了夜间低光条件下的无人机跟踪难题，所提出的DarkSOT基准为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Severe image degradation under low-light nighttime conditions constitutes a core bottleneck preventing all-day applications for UAV-based single object tracking. Existing image enhancement methods often struggle to distinguish between target and background regions, which can easily lead to amplified background noise or compromise target features. To overcome this limitation, we propose TAE, a target-aware low-light enhancement framework tailored for nighttime object tracking. Guided explicitly by weak supervisory signals from tracking bounding boxes, the framework performs region-aware enhancement to ensure operations focus on the target area. It further adopts an adaptive RGB multi-curve fusion mechanism to achieve refined modeling and adaptive adjustment across different regions. To facilitate research in this domain, we also contribute DarkSOT, a new benchmark for nighttime UAV tracking, comprising 268 sequences across 9 target categories. Experimental results on the DarkSOT and UAVDark135 demonstrate that TAE significantly improves tracking performance in low-light nighttime scenarios, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization. The DarkSOT dataset is available at https://github.com/Fu0511/DarkSOT-Dataset.

</details>


### [37] [From General Vision to Reliable Traversability Estimation: Adapting Vision Foundation Models for Unstructured Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29565)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,Dong-Wook Kim,Yeonkyu Lee,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTA框架通过可学习提示注入任务知识、视角多样化训练处理标注歧义、几何知识蒸馏弥合语义-可通行性差距，实现对视觉基础模型的可靠可通行性估计，在多个领域达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的可通行性估计方法面临三个根本挑战：视觉基础模型的任务无关设计导致缺乏专有知识、可通行性标注存在歧义性、语义标签与物理安全性之间存在差距，这些问题严重影响了估计的可靠性。

Method: ViTA包含三个核心组件：1) 可学习可通行性提示注入任务特定知识；2) 视角多样化训练通过估计语义不确定性来抑制歧义边界上的置信预测；3) 几何知识蒸馏使得推理阶段仅从RGB图像推理坡度和高程信息，最终融合语义与几何输出为连续可通行性分数。

Result: 在多个领域（包括真实世界越野数据集）的评估中，ViTA实现了SOTA的IoU和精度，显著降低了假阳性率，并展现出强大的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: ViTA通过任务特定知识注入、标注歧义处理和语义-几何融合，有效解决了现有可通行性估计范式的根本性挑战，为实现可靠、可泛化的无人环境感知提供了新路径。

Abstract: Vision-based approaches have become the dominant paradigm for traversability estimation in unstructured outdoor environments, typically adapting vision foundation models (VFMs) via semantic segmentation supervision. However, this paradigm faces three fundamental challenges that undermine its reliability: the task-agnostic design of VFMs, the ambiguity of traversability annotations, and the discrepancy between semantic labels and physical safety. We propose Vision-to-Traversability Adaptation (ViTA), a framework that adapts VFMs for reliable traversability estimation, instantiated on SAM2. ViTA injects task-specific knowledge through learnable traversability prompts while preserving the VFM's cross-domain generalization. To handle annotation ambiguity, we introduce Perspective-Diversified Training, which estimates semantic uncertainty to suppress confident predictions at ambiguous boundaries. To bridge the semantic-traversability discrepancy, we distill geometric knowledge during training, enabling slope and elevation reasoning from RGB images alone at inference. The semantic and geometric outputs are fused into a continuous traversability score that reflects both semantic uncertainty and geometric risk. Evaluations across diverse domains, including challenging real-world off-road datasets, demonstrate that ViTA achieves state-of-the-art IoU and Precision with substantial false-positive reduction and strong cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [38] [DefSynUS: Real-time Patient-specific Intrahepatic Vessel Identification via Deformation-Aware CT-US Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29570)
*Karl-Philippe Beaudet,Yordanka Velikova,Sidaty El Hadramy,Nassir Navab,Philippe Cattin,Juan Verde,Stéphane Cotin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可变形超声增强的实时、患者特异性肝内血管识别框架，利用术前CT标注通过物理渲染生成合成超声数据，并结合域自适应实现术中实时识别，在体模和单例临床实验中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜超声在肝脏手术中可实时显示肝内血管，但因探头限制、血管结构复杂和组织变形，血管识别仍具挑战。现有方法依赖术前超声或无法适应术中变形，亟需一种无需术前超声、且对变形鲁棒的实时患者特异性血管识别方法。

Method: 利用术前CT血管标注，通过优化的基于物理的渲染生成合成超声数据，并结合域自适应技术将其与术中超声对齐。渲染过程以端到端方式训练，针对血管识别和患者特异性进行优化，无需术前超声。在渲染管线中引入变形感知增强，模拟真实的术中运动和组织变形。

Result: 在腹部体模和单例临床可行性实验中，该框架实现了实时肝内血管分支识别，并在新患者姿态下保持了识别性能。

Conclusion: 该框架无需术前超声即可实现实时血管识别，验证了技术可行性，但仍需多患者验证以确保泛化性和临床可行性。

Abstract: Purpose: Laparoscopic ultrasound (LUS) enhances the safety of liver surgery by visualizing intrahepatic vessels in real-time. Still, vessel identification remains difficult due to probe constraints, complex vascular structure, and tissue deformation. This work aims to enable real-time, patient-specific vessel identification that remains robust under deformation through deformable ultrasound augmentation. Methods: Preoperative CT vessel annotations are used to generate synthetic ultrasound data via optimized physics-based rendering, coupled with domain adaptation to intraoperative ultrasound.
